      "# Library Bug-Fix Report \u2014 lib_bejson_Core_bejson_list_validator.py\n\n**Authorization:** Directive 1, explicit user instruction, this conversation, 2026-07-09.\nThis is the one file under `/libraries/` touched in package version 2 \u2014 everything\nelse in this delivery lives under `library extensions/`.\n\n**File:** `libraries/Lib_PY/Core/lib_bejson_Core_bejson_list_validator.py`\n**Version:** 1.2.0 \u2192 1.3.1\n\n## Change 1 \u2014 Decouple I/O from validation (as directed)\n\n**Before:** `validate_list(doc_path: str)` loaded the file itself via\n`BEJSONCore.bejson_core_load_file(doc_path)`.\n\n**After:** `validate_list(doc_data: dict)` takes an already-loaded document\ndict. The caller (typically `MFDBCore.mfdb_core_get_entity_doc()` or\n`BEJSONCore.bejson_core_load_file()`) is responsible for loading it.\n\n## Change 2 \u2014 Fix the confirmed bug (as directed)\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nif not StandardValidator.bejson_validator_validate_file(doc_path):\n     return {\"is_valid\": False, \"errors\": StandardValidator.bejson_validator_get_errors()}\n```\n`bejson_validator_get_errors()` does not exist anywhere in\n`lib_bejson_Core_bejson_validator.py` \u2014 a genuine structural-validation\nfailure raised `AttributeError` instead of returning a clean result.\n\n**After:**\n```python\nres = StandardValidator.validate_bejson(doc_data, is_file=False)\nif not res.valid:\n    return {\"is_valid\": False, \"errors\": res.errors}\n```\n`validate_bejson()` is a real function that returns a `ValidationResult`\nwith `.valid` (bool) and `.errors` (list[str]). It also accepts dicts\nnatively (`bejson_validator_check_json_syntax` passes a dict straight\nthrough when `is_file=False`), so this change satisfies Change 1 at the\nsame time.\n\nVerified with a deliberately malformed doc (missing `Format_Creator`):\nreturns `{'is_valid': False, 'errors': ['Missing key: Format_Creator']}`\ninstead of raising.\n\n## Change 3 \u2014 Format_Version check (found while implementing Directive 2, not requested, fixed because it blocked the directive)\n\n**Before:**\n```python\nif doc.get(\"Format_Version\") != \"104a\":\n    return {\"is_valid\": False, \"errors\": [\"List Manager requires BEJSON 104a format.\"]}\n```\n\nOnce Category/Nav moved onto the standard `MFDBCore` manifest+entity split\n(Directive 2), their entity docs \u2014 returned by the new\n`MFDBCore.mfdb_core_get_entity_doc()` \u2014 are legitimately `Format_Version:\n\"104\"` (they carry `Parent_Hierarchy`, per the MFDB spec), not `\"104a\"`.\nThe old check rejected every one of them outright, which would have made\nDirective 2 impossible to satisfy.\n\n**After:**\n```python\nif doc.get(\"Format_Version\") not in (\"104a\", \"104\"):\n    return {\"is_valid\": False, \"errors\": [\"List Manager requires BEJSON 104 or 104a format.\"]}\n```\n`\"104\"` was already a recognized valid version in\n`lib_bejson_Core_bejson_validator.py`'s own `VALID_VERSIONS` set \u2014 this\njust stops `validate_list()` from being stricter than the standard\nvalidator it wraps. The hierarchy logic itself is unchanged: it only\ndepends on `id`/`parent_id` being present in the field map, not on which\nof the two valid list-shaped formats the document uses.\n\n## Verification\n\nRan against four cases directly: a valid two-row category tree, a\nduplicate-ID case, an orphaned-parent case, and a deliberately malformed\ndoc missing a mandatory key. All four returned the expected result shape\nwith no exceptions. Also verified end-to-end against real `MFDBCore`\nentity docs for all five `management_cms` taxonomies (Category, Nav, Page,\nPost, Media) \u2014 `management_cms_audit()`'s `mfdb_core_deep_verify()` pass\nreturned zero findings for all five, confirming the fix works against the\nreal entity-doc shape, not just synthetic test docs.\n",

File_Name\n                        f_path.suffix,    # File_Extension\n                        content,          # File_Content\n                        version,          # File_Version\n                        file_hash,        # Session-level mount flag (real boolean); distinct from the per-row Is_Mounted field in Fields[].\n        \"Session_Is_Mounted\": False,\n        \"Mount_Path\": \"\",\n        \"Package_Version\": package_version or \"1\",\n        \"Records_Type\": [\"Chunked\"],\n        \"Fields\": CHUNKED_104_FIELDS,\n        \"Values\": values,\n    }\n\n\n# Restores the files of a Chunked-104a document to output_dir (Is_Binary decides text vs base64 decode). Returns the number of files written. Paths are checked so a row cannot escape output_dir.\ndef bejson_core_chunking_unchunk_chunked_104(doc: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"\n    Restores files from a Chunked-104a document back to disk. Is_Binary picks\n    the decode path per row: True -> base64-decode File_Content back to raw\n    bytes, False -> write File_Content as UTF-8 text. Returns the count of\n    files actually written.\n    \"\"\"\n    fm = {f[\"name\"]: i for i, f in enumerate(doc.get(\"Fields\", CHUNKED_104_FIELDS))}\n    out_root = Path(output_dir).resolve()\n    count = 0\n\n    for row in doc.get(\"Values\", []):\n        rel_path = row[fm[\"Relative_Path\"]]\n        is_binary = row[fm[\"Is_Binary\"]]\n        content = row[fm[\"File_Content\"]]\n        if not rel_path or content is None:\n            continue\n        target_file = Path(bejson_safe_join(str(out_root), rel_path))\n        target_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        if is_binary:\n            target_file.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(content))\n        else:\n            target_file.write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        count += 1\n\n    return count\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500 MFDB 1.32 session-based mount \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Mirrors MFDBArchive (131) exactly, substituting unchunk/rechunk for\n# unzip/rezip. Lock file is .mfdb132_lock. Is_Mounted and Mount_Path\n# top-level headers on the chunk doc are live mount-state markers.\n\nLOCK_FILE_132 = \".mfdb132_lock\"\n\n\n# SHA-256 of a file on disk (streamed). Used for chunk integrity checks.\ndef _calculate_chunk_hash(file_path: str) -> str:\n    sha256 = hashlib.sha256()\n    with open(file_path, \"rb\") as f:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(4096), b\"\"):\n            sha256.update(chunk)\n    return sha256.hexdigest()\n\n\n# Writes the session headers Session_Is_Mounted and Mount_Path into a chunk document on disk. Any in-memory _bejson_field_map key is removed first so the cache is never written to disk.\ndef _set_chunk_doc_headers(chunk_doc_path: str, is_mounted: bool, mount_path: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Update Session_Is_Mounted / Mount_Path headers in a chunk doc file on disk.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with open(chunk_doc_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            doc = json.load(f)\n        doc.pop(\"_bejson_field_map\", None)  # never rewrite an in-memory cache to disk\n        doc[\"Session_Is_Mounted\"] = bool(is_mounted)\n        doc.pop(\"Is_Mounted\", None)  # Session_Is_Mounted is the only document-level mount flag\n        doc[\"Mount_Path\"] = mount_path\n        with open(chunk_doc_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            json.dump(doc, f, indent=2)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.warning(f\"[MFDB132] Could not update chunk doc headers: {e}\")\n\n\nclass MFDB132Archive:\n    \"\"\"\n    Session-based mount for MFDB 1.32 chunked packages.\n    Mirrors MFDBArchive (131) but uses unchunk/rechunk instead of unzip/rezip.\n\n    Lock file:  <workspace>/.mfdb132_lock  (JSON: pid, chunk_doc_path,\n                                             original_hash, workspace_dir, mounted_at)\n    Is_Mounted: Top-level header on the chunk doc \u2014 set True on mount,\n                False on unmount.\n    Mount_Path: Top-level header on the chunk doc \u2014 workspace abs path while\n                mounted, empty string when not.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Chunked-104a session step: extracts the document into a workspace directory, takes a '132' session lock and sets Session_Is_Mounted / Mount_Path. sticky=True reuses valid extracted files.\n    # If the chunk document holds a packaged MFDB (MFDB-132), the workspace is an ordinary MFDB directory (104a manifest + 104 entity files).\n    @staticmethod\n    def mount(chunk_doc_path: str, target_dir: str,\n              force: bool = False, sticky: bool = True) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Unchunk an MFDB132 package to a workspace and create a session lock.\n        sticky=True reuses an existing valid workspace when the chunk doc hash\n        matches. Returns the absolute path to the restored manifest.\n        \"\"\"\n        chunk_p = Path(chunk_doc_path).resolve()\n        if not chunk_p.exists():\n            from lib_bejson_Core_bejson_errors import E_MFDB_CORE_ARCHIVE_ERROR\n            raise ValueError(f\"Chunk doc not found: {chunk_doc_path}\")\n\n        target_p = Path(target_dir)\n        lock_file = target_p / LOCK_FILE_132\n        manifest_path = target_p / \"104a.mfdb.bejson\"\n        current_hash = _calculate_chunk_hash(str(chunk_p))\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Sticky reuse \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        if sticky and lock_file.exists() and manifest_path.exists():\n            try:\n                with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n                    lock_data = json.load(f)\n                if lock_data.get(\"original_hash\") == current_hash:\n                    from lib_bejson_Core_mfdb_validator import mfdb_validator_validate_database\n                    if mfdb_validator_validate_database(str(manifest_path)):\n                        return str(manifest_path.absolute())\n            except Exception:\n                pass  # Fall through to full re-unchunk\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Ownership check \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        if lock_file.exists() and not force:\n            with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n                lock_data = json.load(f)\n            if lock_data.get(\"pid\") != os.getpid():\n                raise PermissionError(\n                    f\"Workspace {target_dir} is locked by PID {lock_data.get('pid')}.\"\n                    \" Pass force=True to override.\"\n                )\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Clear workspace, unchunk \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        if target_p.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(target_dir)\n        target_p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        with open(chunk_p, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            doc = json.load(f)\n\n        count = bejson_core_chunking_unchunk_chunked_104(doc, target_dir)\n        if count == 0:\n            shutil.rmtree(target_dir)\n            raise ValueError(\"Unchunk produced zero files \u2014 chunk doc may be empty.\")\n\n        if not manifest_path.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(target_dir)\n            raise ValueError(\n                \"Invalid MFDB132 package: 104a.mfdb.bejson missing after unchunk.\"\n            )\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Write session lock \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        lock_data = {\n            \"pid\": os.getpid(),\n            \"mounted_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            \"original_hash\": current_hash,\n            \"chunk_doc_path\": str(chunk_p),\n            \"workspace_dir\": str(target_p.absolute()),\n        }\n        with open(lock_file, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(lock_data, f)\n\n        # Re-chunks the mounted workspace back into the chunk document (atomic). With validate=True it refuses to write if validation fails. Returns the output path.\n    @staticmethod\n    def commit(mount_dir: str, output_path: Optional[str] = None,\n               validate: bool = True) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Re-chunk the workspace back into an MFDB132 package atomically.\n        Runs full MFDB validation as a pre-write gate (refuses bad writes before\n        touching disk \u2014 unlike the post-write check in unchunk_mfdb132_package).\n        \"\"\"\n        mount_p = Path(mount_dir)\n        lock_file = mount_p / LOCK_FILE_132\n        manifest_path = mount_p / \"104a.mfdb.bejson\"\n\n        if not lock_file.exists():\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"No active 132 mount session in {mount_dir}\")\n\n        with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n            lock_data = json.load(f)\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Pre-write validation gate \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        if validate:\n            if not manifest_path.exists():\n                raise RuntimeError(\"Commit rejected: manifest missing in workspace.\")\n            from lib_bejson_Core_mfdb_validator import mfdb_validator_validate_database\n            try:\n                mfdb_validator_validate_database(str(manifest_path))\n            except Exception as e:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Commit rejected: validation failed \u2014 {e}\")\n\n        dest_path = output_path or lock_data.get(\"chunk_doc_path\")\n        if not dest_path:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"Destination chunk doc path unknown.\")\n\n        # \u2500\u2500 Rechunk to temp, atomic swap \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n        fd, temp_chunk = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\".mfdb132.bejson\")\n        os.close(fd)\n        try:\n            new_doc = bejson_core_chunking_create_mfdb132_package(mount_dir, db_name)\n            with open(temp_chunk, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                json.dump(new_doc, f, indent=2)\n            shutil.move(temp_chunk, dest_path)\n        except Exception as e:\n            if os.path.exists(temp_chunk):\n                os.remove(temp_chunk)\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Commit failed during rechunk: {e}\")\n\n        # Recovery helper: re-extracts a single file from the chunk document into the mounted workspace.\n    @staticmethod\n    def resurrect_file(mount_dir: str, relative_path: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Re-unchunk a single file from the chunk doc into the workspace.\n        Used for recovery when an entity file is missing or corrupted.\n        No full re-unchunk required.\n        \"\"\"\n        mount_p = Path(mount_dir)\n        lock_file = mount_p / LOCK_FILE_132\n        if not lock_file.exists():\n            return False\n\n        with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n            lock_data = json.load(f)\n\n        chunk_doc_path = lock_data.get(\"chunk_doc_path\")\n        if not chunk_doc_path or not os.path.exists(chunk_doc_path):\n            return False\n\n        try:\n            with open(chunk_doc_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                doc = json.load(f)\n\n            fm = {field[\"name\"]: i\n                  for i, field in enumerate(doc.get(\"Fields\", CHUNKED_104_FIELDS))}\n            target_rel = Path(relative_path)\n\n            for row in doc.get(\"Values\", []):\n                row_rel = Path(row[fm[\"Relative_Path\"]])\n                if row_rel == target_rel:\n                    target_file = Path(bejson_safe_join(str(mount_p), str(row_rel)))\n                    target_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                    is_binary = row[fm[\"Is_Binary\"]]\n                    content   = row[fm[\"File_Content\"]]\n                    if is_binary:\n                        target_file.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(content))\n                    else:\n                        target_file.write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n                    return True\n        except Exception as e:\n            logging.warning(f\"[MFDB132] resurrect_file failed for {relative_path}: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n    # Releases the 132 session lock and clears Session_Is_Mounted / Mount_Path; with cleanup=True also removes the workspace.\n    @staticmethod\n    def unmount(mount_dir: str, cleanup: bool = True) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Release the 132 session lock and clear Is_Mounted / Mount_Path headers\n        on the chunk doc. Both schemas share the same\n# per-file field vocabulary (File_Name/File_Extension/Relative_Path/File_Content/File_Hash/\n# Is_Binary/Is_Mounted) -- rolling MFDB entities just add 'version' on top.\n# This section is the detection + dispatch layer that lets calling code hand\n# it ANY of the four known doc shapes and get the right restore behavior\n# back without knowing which one it is up front. It does not duplicate the\n# flat-schema restore logic above -- bejson_core_chunking_mfdb_unchunk()\n# delegates to bejson_core_chunking_unchunk_chunked_104() for that case.\n#\n# MFDB versioning is handled leniently: MFDB_Version header values are\n# informational only (1.31/1.32/1.38 and any future value all pass) --\n# schema detection is structural (which Fields are present), never a\n# version-string allowlist, so a newer/older MFDB_Version this file hasn't\n# been told about yet still detects correctly.\n\nBEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_MANIFEST      = \"mfdb_manifest\"\nBEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_ENTITY        = \"mfdb_entity\"\nBEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_ENTITY_LEGACY = \"mfdb_entity_legacy\"\nBEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_CHUNKED_104A  = \"chunked_104a\"\nBEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN       = \"unknown\"\n\n# Field names a current MFDB entity row must have.\n_MFDB_ENTITY_FIELD_NAMES = {\n    \"version\", \"File_Name\", \"File_Extension\", \"Relative_Path\",\n    \"File_Content\", \"File_Hash\", \"Is_Binary\", \"Is_Mounted\",\n}\n# Field names of the lowercase MFDB entity schema (see SCHEMA_MFDB_ENTITY_v5); Pure structure check.\ndef bejson_core_chunking_mfdb_detect_schema(doc: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Structural detection across all four known chunk doc shapes.\"\"\"\n    records_type = doc.get(\"Records_Type\")\n    field_names = {f[\"name\"] for f in doc.get(\"Fields\", [])}\n\n    if records_type == [\"MFDB-132\"] or doc.get(\"Schema_Name\") == \"MFDB-132\":\n        return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_CHUNKED_104A\n    if records_type == [\"Chunked\"] and field_names == _CHUNKED_104A_FIELD_NAMES:\n        return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_CHUNKED_104A\n    if field_names == _MFDB_ENTITY_FIELD_NAMES:\n        return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_ENTITY\n    if field_names == _MFDB_ENTITY_LEGACY_FIELD_NAMES:\n        return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_ENTITY_LEGACY\n    if records_type == [\"mfdb\"] and \"entity_name\" in field_names and \"file_path\" in field_names:\n        return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_MANIFEST\n    return BEJSON_CORE_CHUNKING_MFDB_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN\n\n\n# Checks that a chunk document's MFDB package version is one of the known versions (1.31, 1.32, 1.38). Version here is the MFDB package/convention version, not a BEJSON Format_Version.\ndef bejson_core_chunking_mfdb_check_version(\n    doc: Dict[str, Any],\n    known_versions: Tuple[str, ...] = (\"1.31\", \"1.32\", \"1.38\"),\n) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Lenient version check: returns a warning string if MFDB_Version is present\n    but not in the known set, or None if it's known (or absent -- absence\n    isn't an error). Never raises, never blocks processing -- structural\n    detection in bejson_core_chunking_mfdb_detect_schema() is what actually\n    gates behavior, not this.\n    \"\"\"\n    mfdb_version = doc.get(\"MFDB_Version\")\n    if mfdb_version is None:\n        return None\n    if str(mfdb_version) not in known_versions:\n        return (f\"MFDB_Version '{mfdb_version}' not in known set {known_versions} -- \"\n                f\"proceeding on structural detection anyway, but this is worth a look.\")\n    return None\n\n\n# Restores a rolling multi-version MFDB chunk (manifest + entity split, all versions as rows) into a directory; version selects which release to extract.\ndef bejson_core_chunking_mfdb_unchunk(doc: Dict[str, Any], output_dir: str,\n                                       version: Optional[str] = None,\n                                       manifest_dir: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Single entry point for restoring ANY of the four known schemas.\n\n    - CHUNKED_104A: doc is the complete flat bundle; delegates to\n      bejson_core_chunking_unchunk_chunked_104().       "\"\"\"\nLibrary:         lib_bejson_Core_bejson_core.py\nFamily:          Core\nModule Purpose:  Format-level primitives: atomic write with PID lock, file/string loading, field-map cache, and document factories for 104, 104a and 104db.\nArchitecture:    Format-level BEJSON layer that works on any BEJSON document (104, 104a, 104db) through positional Fields/Values and the field-map cache. It does not orchestrate MFDB and is not specific to 104db.\nFormat_Scope:    104 | 104a | 104db (generic)\nGlossary:        BEJSON 104, 104a and 104db are the three file formats (Format_Version). 104db = ONE file holding 2+ entity types\n                 (Record_Type_Parent discriminator, null-padding). MFDB is NOT a format and NOT 104db: it is a layer made of one 104a manifest\n                 (104a.mfdb.bejson) plus dense 104 entity files linked by Parent_Hierarchy. MFDB-132 = an MFDB packed into one Chunked-104a document.\nVersion:         2.0.9\nRelease_Version: 300\nDate:            2026-10-06\nAuthor:          Elton Boehnen \u00b7 boehnenelton2024@gmail.com \u00b7 boehnenelton2024.pages.dev \u00b7 github.com/boehnenelton\nFormat_Creator:  Elton Boehnen\nRELATIONAL_ID:   4e71d63a-88fe-4737-87fd-b4136f6fd9af\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport shutil\nimport tempfile\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union\n\nclass BEJSONCoreError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a BEJSON core operation fails.\"\"\"\n    # Exception type for core-layer failures (class BEJSONCoreError). `code` is an optional numeric error code.\n    def __init__(self, message: str, code: int = None):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.code = code\n\nclass ResilientPIDLock:\n    # Creates the lock handle for target_path: lock directory <path>.lockdir, metadata file lock_meta.json, timeout in seconds. Does not take the lock until acquire()/with.\n    def __init__(self, target_path: Union[str, Path], timeout_seconds: int = 10):\n        self.target    = Path(target_path)\n        self.lock_dir  = Path(f\"{target_path}.lockdir\")\n        self.meta_file = self.lock_dir / \"lock_meta.json\"\n        self.timeout   = timeout_seconds\n\n    # Takes the PID-lock: creates <target>.lockdir (an atomic mkdir) and writes lock_meta.json with the owner PID. Reclaims the lock if the recorded PID is dead. Waits up to `timeout` seconds; returns False on timeout.\n    def acquire(self) -> bool:\n        start_time = time.time()\n        while time.time() - start_time < self.timeout:\n            try:\n                self.lock_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=False)\n                self.meta_file.write_text(json.dumps({\n                    \"pid\":       os.getpid(),\n                    \"timestamp\": int(time.time())\n                }))\n                return True\n            except FileExistsError:\n                if self.meta_file.exists():\n                    try:\n                        meta      = json.loads(self.meta_file.read_text())\n                        owner_pid = meta.get(\"pid\")\n                        if owner_pid:\n                            os.kill(owner_pid, 0)  # Signal 0: check if alive\n                    except (ProcessLookupError, OSError):\n                        # Owner is dead \u2014 safely reclaim\n                        self.release()\n                        continue\n                    except Exception:\n                        pass\n                time.sleep(0.1)\n        return False\n\n    # Removes lock_meta.json and the .lockdir. Safe to call when the lock is already gone.\n    def release(self):\n        if self.meta_file.exists():\n            try:\n                self.meta_file.unlink()\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n        try:\n            self.lock_dir.rmdir()\n        except OSError:\n            pass\n\n    # Context-manager entry: acquires the lock or raises OSError(53) (E_MFDB_CORE_LOCK_FAILED code) on timeout. Used for writes to any BEJSON file, MFDB or not.\n    def __enter__(self):\n        if not self.acquire():\n            raise OSError(53, \"Mutex lock timeout expired (E_MFDB_CORE_LOCK_FAILED)\")\n        return self\n\n    # Context-manager exit: always releases the lock.\n    def __exit__(self, *_):\n        self.release()\n\nfrom lib_bejson_Core_bejson_env import resolve_path\n\n# The field-map cache lives in memory only. It is never read back from disk or from a string.\nFIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY = \"_bejson_field_map\"\n\nclass _FieldMap(dict):\n    \"\"\"Provenance marker for engine-built field maps. A map that arrived via json.load/json.loads is a\n    plain dict and is never trusted; only instances built by get_field_map pass the freshness guard.\"\"\"\n    __slots__ = ()\n\n# Security: removes a persisted _bejson_field_map key from a freshly loaded document and logs a warning. The field-map cache is an in-memory artifact only; a copy that arrives from a file or string is untrusted (it could point field lookups at the wrong column). Called by load_file and load_string.\n# Format-agnostic: applies to 104, 104a, 104db and MFDB manifest/entity files.\ndef bejson_core_strip_persisted_cache(doc: Any) -> Any:\n    \"\"\"Discards a persisted field-map cache from a freshly loaded document (untrusted input).\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(doc, dict) and doc.pop(FIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY, None) is not None:\n        logging.warning(\"[BEJSON_CORE] Discarded persisted %s (untrusted cache)\", FIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY)\n    return doc\n\n# Loads a BEJSON file (any version: 104, 104a, 104db, or an MFDB manifest/entity file) into a dict. Resolves path placeholders first. Returns None if missing or unparseable.\n# Does not validate -- use bejson_validator for that. A persisted _bejson_field_map key in the file is discarded (untrusted cache).\ndef bejson_core_load_file(path: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Loads a BEJSON file and returns the dictionary.\"\"\"\n    path = resolve_path(path)\n    if not path:\n        return None\n    try:\n        with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            return bejson_core_strip_persisted_cache(json.load(f))\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.error(f\"[BEJSON_CORE] Failed to load {path}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n# Writes a BEJSON document atomically (temp file, fsync, rename). Format-agnostic: works for 104, 104a, 104db and MFDB manifest/entity files.\n# Keys that start with '_' are in-memory metadata (e.g. cached field map) and are stripped before writing.\ndef bejson_core_atomic_write(path: str, data: dict) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Writes a BEJSON file atomically using a temp file and sync.\"\"\"\n    target_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path))\n    os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Strip internal metadata keys (starting with _) before write\n    clean_data = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")}\n\n    fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=target_dir, suffix=\".tmp\")\n    try:\n        with os.fdopen(fd, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(clean_data, f, indent=2)\n            f.flush()\n            os.fsync(f.fileno())\n        os.replace(tmp_path, path)\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.error(f\"[BEJSON_CORE] Atomic write failed for {path}: {e}\")\n        if os.path.exists(tmp_path):\n            os.unlink(tmp_path)\n        return False\n\n# Simple directory-based lock for a file path (<path>.lock). Returns True when acquired within `timeout`. Lighter than ResilientPIDLock (no stale-PID recovery).\ndef bejson_core_acquire_lock(file_path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Acquire a simple directory-based lock.\"\"\"\n    lock_path = file_path + \".lock\"\n    start_time = time.time()\n    while time.time() - start_time < timeout:\n        try:\n            os.mkdir(lock_path)\n            return True\n        except FileExistsError:\n            time.sleep(0.1)\n    return False\n\n# Releases the simple directory lock taken by bejson_core_acquire_lock.\ndef bejson_core_release_lock(file_path: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Release the simple directory-based lock.\"\"\"\n    lock_path = file_path + \".lock\"\n    try:\n        os.rmdir(lock_path)\n    except OSError:\n        pass\n\n# Global Field Map Cache\n# Key: tuple of field names (sorted or as-is)\n# Value: dict of {name: index}\n_FIELD_MAP_CACHE: Dict[tuple, Dict[str, int]] = {}\n\n# Name of a Fields entry (the dict's 'name', or str() of a non-dict entry). Used by the freshness guard.\ndef _field_name(field_def: Any) -> Any:\n    return field_def.get(\"name\") if isinstance(field_def, dict) else str(field_def)\n\n# O(1) guard for an in-document field-map cache. Trusts the cache only if (a) it is a _FieldMap instance built by this engine (anything that came from json.load is a plain dict and is rejected), (b) its size equals len(Fields), and (c) the first, middle and last field names resolve to 0, len//2 and len-1.\n# Catches append, pop, list replacement and swapped indices without scanning Fields. Duplicate field names fail the size check, which forces a safe rebuild. In-place edits that keep size and the three probe entries are not detected (blast radius: that one document).\ndef bejson_core_field_map_is_fresh(cached_map: Any, fields: Any) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    O(1) freshness guard for an in-document cache.\n    Accepts the cache only if its size equals len(Fields) and the first, middle and last\n    field names resolve to 0, len//2 and len-1. Catches append, pop, list replacement and\n    swapped indices without scanning Fields. Duplicate field names always fail (size\n    mismatch), which forces a safe rebuild instead of a silent wrong index.\n    \"\"\"\n    if type(cached_map) is not _FieldMap or not isinstance(fields, list):\n        return False  # only engine-built maps are trusted; parsed/forged plain dicts are rebuilt\n    count = len(fields)\n    if len(cached_map) != count:\n        return False\n    if count == 0:\n        return True\n    last = count - 1\n    mid = count // 2\n    return (cached_map.get(_field_name(fields[0])) == 0\n            and cached_map.get(_field_name(fields[last])) == last\n            and cached_map.get(_field_name(fields[mid])) == mid)\n\n# Returns {field_name: column_index} for a BEJSON document, built once from Fields and cached on the document and in a global cache. All field access should go through this (no hardcoded positions).\n# Works for 104, 104a, and 104db. In a 104db document, column 0 is the Record_Type_Parent discriminator.\n# The in-document cache is trusted only when it was built by this engine and is still fresh (bejson_core_field_map_is_fresh);\n# otherwise it is rebuilt from Fields. Each document receives its own copy of the shared map, so one caller's edits cannot affect other documents.\ndef bejson_core_get_field_map(doc: dict) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns a mapping of field name to index.\n    The in-document cache is reused only after the O(1) freshness guard passes; a stale or\n    forged map is discarded and rebuilt. The global cache is never handed out: each document\n    receives its own copy (one O(F) copy on first access), so a caller mutating the returned\n    map cannot corrupt other documents that share the same schema. Repeat calls stay O(1).\n    \"\"\"\n    fields = doc.get(\"Fields\", [])\n    cached_map = doc.get(FIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY)\n    if cached_map is not None:\n        if bejson_core_field_map_is_fresh(cached_map, fields):\n            return cached_map\n        doc.pop(FIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY, None)  # stale or poisoned: discard\n\n    if not fields or not isinstance(fields, list):\n        return {}\n\n    field_names = tuple(_field_name(f) for f in fields)\n    cache_key = (doc.get(\"Format_Version\"), field_names)\n\n    shared_map = _FIELD_MAP_CACHE.get(cache_key)\n    if shared_map is None:\n        shared_map = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(field_names)}\n        _FIELD_MAP_CACHE[cache_key] = shared_map\n\n    field_map = _FieldMap(shared_map)\n    try:\n        doc[FIELD_MAP_CACHE_KEY] = field_map\n    except Exception:\n        pass  # In case doc is immutable or not a dict\n\n    return field_map\n\n# Column index of one field name via the field-map cache; -1 if the field does not exist.\ndef bejson_core_get_field_index(doc: dict, field_name: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Returns the positional index of a field name using the freshness-guarded cache.\"\"\"\n    return bejson_core_get_field_map(doc).get(field_name, -1)\n\n# Factory: new BEJSON 104 document -- exactly ONE record type, strict (no custom top-level keys). Also the format of every MFDB entity file (the caller adds Parent_Hierarchy).\ndef bejson_core_create_104(record_type: str, fields: list, values: list) -> dict:\n    return {\n        \"Format\": \"BEJSON\",\n        \"Format_Version\": \"104\",\n        \"Format_Creator\": \"Elton Boehnen\",\n        \"Records_Type\": [record_type],\n        \"Fields\": fields,\n        \"Values\": values\n    }\n\n# Factory: new BEJSON 104a document -- one record type plus optional custom top-level header keys (**custom), PascalCase recommended. Also the format of the MFDB manifest.\ndef bejson_core_create_104a(record_type: str, fields: list, values: list, **custom) -> dict:\n    doc = {\n        \"Format\": \"BEJSON\",\n        \"Format_Version\": \"104a\",\n        \"Format_Creator\": \"Elton Boehnen\",\n        \"Records_Type\": [record_type],\n        \"Fields\": fields,\n        \"Values\": values\n    }\n    doc.update(custom)\n    return doc\n\n# Factory: new BEJSON 104db document -- ONE file holding 2+ record types. record_types lists the entities; fields must include Record_Type_Parent first and per-field Record_Type_Parent ownership; unrelated cells are null-padded.\n# This is the single-file multi-entity format. It is not MFDB.\ndef bejson_core_create_104db(record_types: list, fields: list, values: list) -> dict:\n    return {\n        \"Format\": \"BEJSON\",\n        \"Format_Version\": \"104db\",\n        \"Format_Creator\": \"Elton Boehnen\",\n        \"Records_Type\": record_types,\n        \"Fields\": fields,\n        \"Values\": values\n    }\n\n# --- Format-level helpers used by mfdb_core and the 104db parser (they work on any BEJSON document) ---\n\n# Parses a JSON string into a dict (any BEJSON version). Logs and returns None on error. No BEJSON validation.\n# A persisted _bejson_field_map key in the text is discarded (untrusted cache).\ndef bejson_core_load_string(content: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n    try:\n        return bejson_core_strip_persisted_cache(json.loads(content))\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.error(f\"[BEJSON_CORE] Failed to load JSON string: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n# Number of rows in Values.\ndef bejson_core_get_record_count(doc: dict) -> int:\n    return len(doc.get(\"Values\", []))\n\n# Appends a row if its length equals the field count; returns False otherwise. The manifest's record_count is a convention that must match the entity file's real row count.\ndef _update_manifest_record_count(\n    manifest_path: str, entity_name: str, count: int\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write a corrected record_count into the manifest for one entity.\"\"\"\n    doc        = _load_json(manifest_path)\n    fn_list    = [f[\"name\"] for f in doc[\"Fields\"]]\n    if \"record_count\" not in fn_list or \"entity_name\" not in fn_list:\n        return\n    rc_idx = fn_list.index(\"record_count\")\n    en_idx = fn_list.index(\"entity_name\")\n    for row in doc[\"Values\"]:\n        if row[en_idx] == entity_name:\n            row[rc_idx] = count\n            break\n    _write_manifest_doc(doc, manifest_path)\n\n# SHA-256 of a file, used for archive integrity checks (corruption detection, not a signature).\ndef _calculate_file_hash(file_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate SHA-256 hash for archive integrity checks.\"\"\"\n    sha256 = hashlib.sha256()\n    with open(file_path, \"rb\") as f:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(4096), b\"\"):\n            sha256.update(chunk)\n    return sha256.hexdigest()\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# MFDBArchive (v1.2 Feature)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass MFDBArchive:\n    \"\"\"\n    Handles .mfdb.zip packaging, virtual mounting, and atomic repacking.\n    Standardized in MFDB v1.2 for portable transport.\n    Enhanced for CoreEvolution with sticky mounting and validation safety.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # MFDB archive step: extracts a .mfdb.zip (a whole MFDB -- manifest + entity files -- packed in one archive) into a workspace directory and writes a session lock (.mfdb_lock).\n    # sticky=True reuses already-extracted valid files. The workspace is an ordinary MFDB directory; the archive is only a container.\n    @staticmethod\n    def mount(archive_path: str, target_dir: str, force: bool = False, sticky: bool = True) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Extract an MFDB archive to a workspace and create a session lock.\n        If sticky=True, it reuses existing valid extracted files.\n        Returns the absolute path to the extracted manifest.\n        \"\"\"\n        arc_p = Path(archive_path)\n        if not arc_p.exists():\n            raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Archive not found: {archive_path}\", E_MFDB_CORE_ARCHIVE_ERROR)\n\n        target_p = Path(target_dir)\n        lock_file = target_p / \".mfdb_lock\"\n        manifest_path = target_p / \"104a.mfdb.bejson\"\n\n        # Sticky check: If valid files exist and hash matches, just return manifest\n        if sticky and lock_file.exists() and manifest_path.exists():\n            try:\n                with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n                    lock_data = json.load(f)\n                \n                # Check if archive hash matches the one we mounted\n                current_arc_hash = _calculate_file_hash(archive_path)\n                if lock_data.get(\"original_hash\") == current_arc_hash:\n                    # Validate the database structure before trusting the sticky mount\n                    from lib_bejson_Core_mfdb_validator import mfdb_validator_validate_database\n                    if mfdb_validator_validate_database(str(manifest_path)):\n                        return str(manifest_path.absolute())\n            except Exception:\n                pass # Fall through to full re-extract if sticky fails\n\n        if lock_file.exists() and not force:\n            with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n                lock_data = json.load(f)\n            if lock_data.get(\"pid\") != os.getpid():\n                raise MFDBCoreError(\n                    f\"Workspace {target_dir} is already locked by PID {lock_data.get('pid')}\",\n                    E_MFDB_CORE_MOUNT_CONFLICT\n                )\n\n        # Clear existing workspace if it was invalid or if we are forcing re-extract\n        if target_p.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(target_dir)\n        target_p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path, 'r') as zip_ref:\n            # Secure extraction loop to mitigate Zip Slip.\n            from lib_bejson_Core_bejson_path_guard import bejson_safe_join\n            for member in zip_ref.namelist():\n                # Skip directories as safe_join/open will handle them\n                if member.endswith('/'): continue\n                \n                # Boundary check via safe_join\n                try:\n                    safe_path = bejson_safe_join(target_dir, member)\n                    # Ensure parent directory exists\n                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(safe_path), exist_ok=True)\n                    with zip_ref.open(member) as source, open(safe_path, \"wb\") as target:\n                        shutil.copyfileobj(source, target)\n                except ValueError as e:\n                    logging.error(f\"[MFDB_CORE] Security Alert: {e}\")\n                    raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Secure extraction failed: {e}\", E_MFDB_CORE_ARCHIVE_ERROR)\n\n        if not manifest_path.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(target_dir)\n            raise MFDBCoreError(\"Invalid MFDB Archive: 104a.mfdb.bejson missing.\", E_MFDB_CORE_ARCHIVE_ERROR)\n\n        # Create session lock with metadata\n        lock_data = {\n            \"pid\": os.getpid(),\n            \"mounted_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            \"original_hash\": _calculate_file_hash(archive_path),\n            \"archive_path\": str(arc_p.absolute())\n        }\n        with open(lock_file, \"w\") as f:\n            json.dump(lock_data, f)\n\n        return str(manifest_path.absolute())\n\n    # Repacks the mounted workspace into the .mfdb.zip atomically. With validate=True it refuses to write when MFDB validation fails.\n    @staticmethod\n    def commit(mount_dir: str, archive_path: Optional[str] = None, validate: bool = True) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Repack the workspace into a .mfdb.zip file atomically.\n        Refuses to write if validation fails (if validate=True).\n        \"\"\"\n        mount_p = Path(mount_dir)\n        lock_file = mount_p / \".mfdb_lock\"\n        manifest_path = mount_p / \"104a.mfdb.bejson\"\n        \n        if not lock_file.exists():\n            raise MFDBCoreError(f\"No active mount session found in {mount_dir}\", E_MFDB_CORE_INVALID_OPERATION)\n\n        if validate:\n            if not manifest_path.exists():\n                raise MFDBCoreError(\"Commit rejected: Manifest missing in workspace.\", E_MFDB_CORE_WRITE_FAILED)\n            \n            # Run full database validation before repacking\n            from lib_bejson_Core_mfdb_validator import mfdb_validator_validate_database\n            try:\n                mfdb_validator_validate_database(str(manifest_path))\n            except Exception as e:\n                raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Commit rejected: Validation failed. {str(e)}\", E_MFDB_CORE_WRITE_FAILED)\n\n        with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n            lock_data = json.load(f)\n\n        dest_path = archive_path or lock_data.get(\"archive_path\")\n        if not dest_path:\n            raise MFDBCoreError(\"Destination archive path unknown.\", E_MFDB_CORE_ARCHIVE_ERROR)\n\n        # Create new archive in temp location\n        fd, temp_arc = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\".mfdb.zip\")\n        os.close(fd)\n\n        try:\n            with zipfile.ZipFile(temp_arc, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:\n                for root, dirs, files in os.walk(mount_dir):\n                    for file in files:\n                        if file == \".mfdb_lock\": continue\n                        file_path = Path(root) / file\n                        arc_name = file_path.relative_to(mount_dir)\n                        zipf.write(file_path, arc_name)\n            \n            # Atomic swap\n            shutil.move(temp_arc, dest_path)\n            \n            # Update lock with new hash to maintain sticky state\n            lock_data[\"original_hash\"] = _calculate_file_hash(dest_path)\n            with open(lock_file, \"w\") as f:\n                json.dump(lock_data, f)\n                \n        except Exception as e:\n            if os.path.exists(temp_arc): os.remove(temp_arc)\n            raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Commit failed: {str(e)}\", E_MFDB_CORE_WRITE_FAILED)\n\n        return dest_path\n\n    # Recovery helper: pulls one file (e.g. a missing or corrupt entity file) back out of the .mfdb.zip into the mounted workspace.\n    @staticmethod\n    def resurrect_file(mount_dir: str, relative_path: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Surgically extract a single file from the .mfdb.zip archive into the workspace.\n        Used for recovery when an entity file is missing or corrupted.\n        \"\"\"\n        mount_p = Path(mount_dir)\n        lock_file = mount_p / \".mfdb_lock\"\n        if not lock_file.exists():\n            return False\n\n        with open(lock_file, \"r\") as f:\n            lock_data = json.load(f)\n        \n        archive_path = zip_ref:\n                # Check if file exists in zip\n                if relative_path in zip_ref.namelist():\n                    zip_ref.extract(relative_path, mount_dir)\n                    return True\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return False\n\n    # Releases the session lock and, with cleanup=True, deletes the extracted workspace.\n    @staticmethod\n    def unmount(mount_dir: str, cleanup: bool = True):\n        \"\"\"Release the lock and optionally delete the workspace.\"\"\"\n        mount_p = Path(mount_dir)\n        lock_file = mount_p / \".mfdb_lock\"\n        if lock_file.exists():\n            os.remove(lock_file)\n        if cleanup and mount_p.exists():\n            shutil.rmtree(mount_dir)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Discovery\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Classifies any file by its MFDB role: 'manifest' (104a named *.mfdb.bejson), 'entity' (104 with Parent_Hierarchy), 'archive' (.mfdb.zip) or 'standalone' (anything else, including a 104db file).\n# A 104db file is never reported as 'manifest' or 'entity'.\ndef mfdb_core_discover(file_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Identify the MFDB role of any file.\n    Returns one of: 'manifest', 'entity', 'archive', 'standalone'\n    \"\"\"\n    p = Path(file_path)\n    if not p.exists():\n        raise MFDBCoreError(f\"File not found: {file_path}\", E_MFDB_CORE_MANIFEST_NOT_FOUND)\n\n    if p.suffix == \".zip\" and \".mfdb\" in p.name:\n        return \"archive\"\n\n    try:\n        doc = _load_json(file_path)\n    except Exception:\n        return \"standalone\"\n\n    version  = doc.get(\"Format_Version\", \"\")\n    filename = p.name\n    if version == \"104a\" and filename.endswith(\".mfdb.bejson\"):\n        return \"manifest\"\n    if version == \"104\" and doc.get(\"Parent_Hierarchy\"):\n        return \"entity\"\n    return \"standalone\"\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Recovery & Repair (v1.21 Feature)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Full audit of an MFDB: manifest valid, every entity file present and valid, Parent_Hierarchy pointing back to the manifest, record_count matching real rows, and orphan files.\n# Returns a list of finding dicts; an empty list means the MFDB is healthy.\ndef mfdb_core_deep_verify(manifest_path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Performs a deep audit of the entire MFDB database.\n    Checks for:\n      - Positional integrity (field vs value length)\n      - Type adherence (basic primitives)\n      - Manifest-entity consistency (record counts)\n      - Foreign key potential breakage (optional warnings)\n    Returns a list of finding dicts.\n    \"\"\"\n    findings = []\n    manifest_doc = bejson_core_load_file(manifest_path)\n    entries = _rows_as_dicts(manifest_doc)\n    \n    for entry in entries:\n        entity_name = entry.get(\"entity_name\")\n        file_path_rel = entry.get(\"file_path\")\n        expected_count = entry.get(\"record_count\")\n        \n        entity_path = _resolve_entity_path(manifest_path, file_path_rel)\n        if not os.path.exists(entity_path):\n            findings.append({\"entity\": entity_name, \"error\": \"MISSING_FILE\", \"path\": file_path_rel})\n            continue\n            \n        try:\n            entity_doc = bejson_core_load_file(entity_path)\n            # 1. Check positional integrity\n            fields = entity_doc.get(\"Fields\", [])\n            field_count = len(fields)\n            values = entity_doc.get(\"Values\", [])\n            actual_count = len(values)\n            \n            if expected_count is not None and expected_count != actual_count:\n                findings.append({\n                    \"entity\": entity_name, \n                    \"warning\": \"COUNT_MISMATCH\", \n                    \"expected\": expected_count, \n                    \"actual\": actual_count\n                })\n            \n            for i, row in enumerate(values):\n                if len(row) != field_count:\n                    findings.append({\n                        \"entity\": entity_name, \n                        \"error\": \"POSITIONAL_VIOLATION\", \n                        \"row\": i, \n                        \"expected\": field_count, \n                        \"actual\": len(row)\n                    })\n                \n                # 2. Basic Type verification\n                for j, val in enumerate(row):\n                    if val is None: continue\n                    f_type = fields[j].get(\"type\")\n                    if f_type == \"integer\" and not isinstance(val, int):\n                         findings.append({\"entity\": entity_name, \"warning\": \"TYPE_MISMATCH\", \"row\": i, \"field\": fields[j][\"name\"], \"expected\": \"integer\", \"actual\": type(val).__name__})\n                    elif f_type == \"number\" and not isinstance(val, (int, float)):\n                         findings.append({\"entity\": entity_name, \"warning\": \"TYPE_MISMATCH\", \"row\": i, \"field\": fields[j][\"name\"], \"expected\": \"number\", \"actual\": type(val).__name__})\n                    elif f_type == \"boolean\" and not isinstance(val, bool):\n                         findings.append({\"entity\": entity_name, \"warning\": \"TYPE_MISMATCH\", \"row\": i, \"field\": fields[j][\"name\"], \"expected\": \"boolean\", \"actual\": type(val).__name__})\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            findings.append({\"entity\": entity_name, \"error\": \"CORRUPT_JSON\", \"message\": str(e)})\n            \n    return findings\n\n# Applies safe automatic fixes for deep_verify findings, one finding at a time (re-sync record counts, repair Parent_Hierarchy, etc.). Returns a result dict describing what was fixed and what failed.\ndef mfdb_core_self_heal(manifest_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Attempts to fix common issues identified by deep_verify.\n    Actions:\n      - Resyncs record_count in manifest.\n      - Padds short records with nulls (Positional Repair).\n      - Removes invalid records if necessary (Extreme measure).\n    Returns a report of actions taken.\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\"actions\": [], \"remaining_errors\": []}\n    findings = mfdb_core_deep_verify(manifest_path)\n    \n    needs_manifest_sync = False\n    \n    for f in findings:\n        entity = f.get(\"entity\")\n        try:\n            if f.get(\"warning\") == \"COUNT_MISMATCH\":\n                # 'actual' may be absent from a finding; skip that finding and continue the pass.\n                actual = f.get(\"actual\")\n                if actual is None:\n                    report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(\n                        f\"Skipped COUNT_MISMATCH heal for {entity}: finding has no 'actual' value\"\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _update_manifest_record_count(manifest_path, entity, actual)\n                report[\"actions\"].append(f\"Resynced record_count for {entity} to {actual}\")\n\n            elif f.get(\"error\") == \"POSITIONAL_VIOLATION\":\n                # Attempt repair\n                entity_path = _get_entity_path(manifest_path, entity)\n                try:\n                    doc = bejson_core_load_file(entity_path)\n                    field_count = len(doc[\"Fields\"])\n                    repaired = 0\n                    for i, row in enumerate(doc[\"Values\"]):\n                        if len(row) < field_count:\n                            doc[\"Values\"][i] = row + [None] * (field_count - len(row))\n                            repaired += 1\n                        elif len(row) > field_count:\n                            doc[\"Values\"][i] = row[:field_count]\n                            repaired += 1\n                    if repaired > 0:\n                        bejson_core_atomic_write(entity_path, doc)\n                        report[\"actions\"].append(f\"Repaired {repaired} positional violations in {entity}\")\n                except Exception as e:\n                    report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(f\"Failed to repair {entity}: {str(e)}\")\n\n            elif f.get(\"error\") == \"MISSING_FILE\":\n                # Attempt resurrection\n                mount_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(manifest_path))\n                fpath = f.get(\"path\")\n                if fpath is None:\n                    report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(f\"Skipped resurrection for {entity}: finding has no 'path' value\")\n                    continue\n                if MFDBArchive.resurrect_file(mount_dir, fpath):\n                    report[\"actions\"].append(f\"Resurrected missing entity file: {fpath}\")\n                else:\n                    report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(f\"Could not resurrect {entity}\")\n\n            elif f.get(\"error\"):\n                report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(f\"{entity}: {f.get('error')} - {f.get('message', '')}\")\n        except Exception as e:\n            # An unexpected finding shape is reported as a heal failure and the loop continues.\n            report[\"remaining_errors\"].append(f\"Unexpected error healing finding for {entity}: {e}\")\n\n    return report\n\n# Rewrites only the Parent_Hierarchy header of a BEJSON 104 entity file so it points at the manifest again.\ndef _mfdb_core_repair_hierarchy(entity_path: str, new_hierarchy: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Surgically update the Parent_Hierarchy header in a BEJSON 104 file.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        doc = bejson_core_load_file(entity_path)\n        doc[\"Parent_Hierarchy\"] = new_hierarchy\n        bejson_core_atomic_write(entity_path, doc)\n        return True\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n# Tries to repair the workspace from a specific MFDBValidationError (e.g. wrong Parent_Hierarchy, stale record_count). Returns True if a repair was applied.\ndef mfdb_core_smart_repair(manifest_path: str, error: MFDBValidationError) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Attempt to automatically repair the MFDB workspace based on a validation error.\n    Supported:\n      - E_MFDB_ENTITY_NOT_FOUND (33): Resurrects from archive.\n      - E_MFDB_BIDIRECTIONAL_FAIL (38) / E_MFDB_MANIFEST_NOT_FOUND (37): Patches Parent_Hierarchy.\n    \"\"\"\n    mount_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(manifest_path))\n    ctx = error.context\n\n    if error.code == E_MFDB_ENTITY_NOT_FOUND or error.code == 33:\n        rel_path = ctx.get(\"file_path_rel\")\n        if rel_path:\n            return MFDBArchive.resurrect_file(mount_dir, rel_path)\n\n    if error.code == E_MFDB_BIDIRECTIONAL_FAIL or error.code == E_MFDB_MANIFEST_NOT_FOUND:\n        entity_path = ctx.get(\"actual_path\")\n        new_hierarchy = ctx.get(\"suggested_hierarchy\")\n        # If suggested_hierarchy is missing but we are in a mount_dir, \n        # assume standard v1.21 structure\n        if not new_hierarchy and entity_path:\n             new_hierarchy = \"../104a.mfdb.bejson\"\n\n        if entity_path and new_hierarchy:\n            return _mfdb_core_repair_hierarchy(entity_path, new_hierarchy)\n\n    return False\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Read operations\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Validates and loads the 104a manifest; returns every registered-entity row as a field-name keyed dict.\ndef mfdb_core_load_manifest(manifest_path: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate and load the manifest.\n    Returns all manifest records as a list of field-name-keyed dicts.\n    \"\"\"\n    mfdb_validator_validate_manifest(manifest_path)\n    doc = _load_json(manifest_path)\n    if not isinstance(doc, dict):\n        raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Failed to load manifest: {manifest_path} (not a dict)\", E_MFDB_CORE_MANIFEST_NOT_FOUND)\n    return _rows_as_dicts(doc)\n\n# Loads all records of one entity as field-name keyed dicts. Rows are dense (no null-padding, no Record_Type_Parent) because an entity file is a single-type BEJSON 104 document.\ndef mfdb_core_load_entity(manifest_path: str, entity_name: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Load all records for a named entity.\n    Returns a list of field-name-keyed dicts (dense - no null-padding).\n    When Debug_Reads is enabled, logs a READ entry to the meta entity.\n    \"\"\"\n    _t0 = time.monotonic()\n    doc = _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    if not isinstance(doc, dict):\n        raise MFDBCoreError(f\"Failed to load entity: {entity_name} (not a dict)\", E_MFDB_CORE_ENTITY_NOT_FOUND)\n    result = _rows_as_dicts(doc)\n    _mfdb_meta_log(\n        manifest_path, \"READ\", entity_name,\n        field_name=None, field_exists=None,\n        row_index=None, success=True,\n        duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - _t0) * 1000),\n        notes=f\"rows_returned={len(result)}\",\n        reads_only=True,\n    )\n    return result\n\n# Returns the raw BEJSON 104 document (dict) of an entity file, for callers that need Fields/Values directly.\ndef mfdb_core_get_entity_doc(manifest_path: str, entity_name: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Return the raw BEJSON 104 document dict for a named entity.\"\"\"\n    return _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n\n# Summary of the whole MFDB: entity count, record counts per entity, sizes. Read-only.\ndef mfdb_core_get_stats(manifest_path: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Return a summary statistics dict for the entire MFDB.\"\"\"\n    doc     = _load_json(manifest_path)\n    entries = _rows_as_dicts(doc)\n\n    entity_stats = []\n    for entry in entries:\n        resolved = _resolve_entity_path(manifest_path, entry[\"file_path\"])\n        if os.path.exists(resolved):\n            edoc        = _load_json(resolved)\n            rec_count   = len(edoc.get(\"Values\", []))\n            field_count = len(edoc.get(\"Fields\", []))\n        else:\n            rec_count   = -1\n            field_count = -1\n\n        entity_stats.append({\n            \"entity_name\":  entry[\"entity_name\"],\n            \"file_path\":    entry[\"file_path\"],\n            \"record_count\": rec_count,\n            \"field_count\":  field_count,\n            \"primary_key\":  entry.get(\"primary_key\"),\n        })\n\n    return {\n        \"db_name\":        doc.get(\"DB_Name\", \"\"),\n        \"schema_version\": doc.get(\"Schema_Version\", \"\"),\n        \"entity_count\":   len(entries),\n        \"entities\":       entity_stats,\n    }\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Query operations\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Returns the entity rows (as dicts) for which predicate(row) is True. Single-entity filter; no cross-entity logic.\ndef mfdb_core_query_entity(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name: str,\n    predicate: Callable[[dict], bool],\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Return all records from an entity for which predicate(record) is True.\"\"\"\n    records = mfdb_core_load_entity(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    return [r for r in records if predicate(r)]\n\n# Builds an in-memory lookup {field value -> row} over one field of one entity for fast access. Not persisted.\ndef mfdb_core_build_index(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name: str,\n    field_name: str,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build an in-memory hash index on a field for fast lookups.\"\"\"\n    records = mfdb_core_load_entity(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    return {r[field_name]: r for r in records if r.get(field_name) is not None}\n\n# Application-level join of two entities on a *_fk-style field. MFDB does not enforce foreign keys; this reads both entity files and matches values.\ndef mfdb_core_join(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    from_entity:   str,\n    to_entity:     str,\n    from_fk:       str,\n    to_pk:         str,\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Cross-entity equi-join.\"\"\"\n    from_records = mfdb_core_load_entity(manifest_path, from_entity)\n    to_index     = mfdb_core_build_index(manifest_path, to_entity, to_pk)\n\n    results = []\n    for record in from_records:\n        fk_val = record.get(from_fk)\n        target = to_index.get(fk_val, {})\n        merged = dict(record)\n        for k, v in target.items():\n            merged[f\"{to_entity}__{k}\"] = v\n        results.append(merged)\n\n    return results\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Write operations\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Appends one row (list matching the entity's Fields order) to an entity file, then syncs the manifest record_count. Row length must equal the field count.\ndef mfdb_core_add_entity_record(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name:   str,\n    values:        list,\n    sync_count:    bool = True,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Append a record to an entity file. Lock held for the full read-modify-write cycle.\"\"\"\n    _t0 = time.monotonic()\n    _success = False\n    entity_path = _get_entity_path(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    try:\n        with ResilientPIDLock(entity_path, timeout_seconds=10):\n            doc = _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n            if not bejson_core_add_record(doc, values):\n                raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Failed to add record to {entity_name}\")\n            _write_entity_doc(doc, entity_path)\n            if sync_count:\n                _update_manifest_record_count(manifest_path, entity_name, len(doc[\"Values\"]))\n        _success = True\n        return doc\n    finally:\n        _mfdb_meta_log(\n            manifest_path, \"ADD\", entity_name,\n            field_name=None, field_exists=None,\n            row_index=None, success=_success,\n            duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - _t0) * 1000),\n            notes=\"\" if _success else \"add_record returned False\",\n        )\n\n# Removes the row at record_index from an entity file and syncs the manifest record_count.\ndef mfdb_core_remove_entity_record(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name:   str,\n    record_index:  int,\n    sync_count:    bool = True,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Remove a record at record_index from an entity file. Lock held for the full read-modify-write cycle.\"\"\"\n    _t0 = time.monotonic()\n    _success = False\n    entity_path = _get_entity_path(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    try:\n        with ResilientPIDLock(entity_path, timeout_seconds=10):\n            doc = _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n            if not bejson_core_remove_record(doc, record_index):\n                raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Failed to remove record {record_index} from {entity_name}\")\n            _write_entity_doc(doc, entity_path)\n            if sync_count:\n                _update_manifest_record_count(manifest_path, entity_name, len(doc[\"Values\"]))\n        _success = True\n        return doc\n    finally:\n        _mfdb_meta_log(\n            manifest_path, \"REMOVE\", entity_name,\n            field_name=None, field_exists=None,\n            row_index=record_index, success=_success,\n            duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - _t0) * 1000),\n            notes=\"\",\n        )\n\n# Updates a single field of one row (by field name) in an entity file. The name is resolved through the field map -- no hardcoded positions.\ndef mfdb_core_update_entity_record(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name:   str,\n    record_index:  int,\n    field_name:    str,\n    new_value:     Any,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Update a single named field in a specific record. Lock held for the full read-modify-write cycle.\"\"\"\n    _t0 = time.monotonic()\n    _success = False\n    _field_exists = None\n    entity_path = _get_entity_path(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    try:\n        with ResilientPIDLock(entity_path, timeout_seconds=10):\n            doc = _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n            if not isinstance(doc, dict):\n                raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Malformed entity doc for {entity_name}\")\n            _field_exists = any(f[\"name\"] == field_name for f in doc.get(\"Fields\", []))\n            if not bejson_core_update_field(doc, record_index, field_name, new_value):\n                raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Failed to update field '{field_name}' in {entity_name}\")\n            _write_entity_doc(doc, entity_path)\n        _success = True\n        return doc\n    finally:\n        _mfdb_meta_log(\n            manifest_path, \"UPDATE\", entity_name,\n            field_name=field_name, field_exists=_field_exists,\n            row_index=record_index, success=_success,\n            duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - _t0) * 1000),\n            notes=\"\" if _field_exists else f\"SCHEMA DRIFT: field '{field_name}' not in Fields[]\",\n        )\n\n# Updates several fields of one row in a single atomic write. Takes a field-name -> value mapping.\ndef mfdb_core_update_entity_record_bulk(\n    manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name:   str,\n    record_index:  int,\n    updates:       Dict[str, Any],\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Update multiple named fields in a specific record. Lock held for the full read-modify-write cycle.\"\"\"\n    _t0 = time.monotonic()\n    _success = False\n    _missing_fields: list = []\n    entity_path = _get_entity_path(manifest_path, entity_name)\n    try:\n        with ResilientPIDLock(entity_path, timeout_seconds=10):\n            doc = _load_entity_doc(manifest_path, entity_name)\n            if not isinstance(doc, dict):\n                raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Malformed entity doc for {entity_name}\")\n            _known = {f[\"name\"] for f in doc.get(\"Fields\", [])}\n            _missing_fields = [k for k in updates if k not in _known]\n            for field_name, new_value in updates.items():\n                if not bejson_core_update_field(doc, record_index, field_name, new_value):\n                    raise BEJSONCoreError(f\"Failed to update field '{field_name}' in {entity_name}\")\n            _write_entity_doc(doc, entity_path)\n        _success = True\n        return doc\n    finally:\n        _notes = (f\"SCHEMA DRIFT: unknown fields {_missing_fields}\" if _missing_fields else \"\")\n        _mfdb_meta_log(\n            manifest_path, \"UPDATE_BULK\", entity_name,\n            field_name=\",\".join(updates.keys()),\n            field_exists=(len(_missing_fields) == 0),\n            row_index=record_index, success=_success,\n            duration_ms=int((time.monotonic() - _t0) * 1000),\n            notes=_notes,\n        )\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Manifest sync\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Counts the real rows of one entity file and writes that number into the manifest. {PROJECTS_ROOT}, \n             internal_storage, ~, and environment variables in ${VAR} format.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not path_str:\n        return path_str\n    \n    from lib_bejson_Core_bejson_env import resolve_path\n    return resolve_path(path_str)\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500 Federated Master / Slave node system \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Network_Role (\"Master\" | \"Slave\") is already emitted on manifest creation\n# (mfdb_core_create_database). This block wires the full runtime protocol:\n#   - ConnectedSlave entity schema + creator (Master side)\n#   - mfdb_federation_push_config   \u2014 Master atomically drops a 104a doc into\n#                                      a Slave's local dropzone directory\n#   - mfdb_federation_poll_dropzone \u2014 Slave polls its own dropzone for incoming\n#                                      Master configs\n#   - mfdb_federation_distill_logs  \u2014 Slave truncates overflow entity rows and\n#                                      pushes a distilled summary to Master\n\nCONNECTED_SLAVE_SCHEMA: List[Dict[str, str]] = [\n    {\"name\": \"slave_id\",           \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"label\",              \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"url\",                \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"role\",               \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"status\",             \"type\": \"string\"},\n    {\"name\": \"supported_entities\", \"type\": \"array\"},\n]\n\n\n# Federation: on a Master manifest (Network_Role 'Master') registers a ConnectedSlave entity and creates its entity file. Raises if the node is not a Master.\ndef mfdb_core_create_connected_slave_entity(manifest_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Register a ConnectedSlave entity in the Master manifest and create its\n    entity file. Raises if the node is not a Master (Network_Role check).\n    Returns the created entity file path.\n    \"\"\"\n    manifest_doc = _load_json(manifest_path)\n    role = manifest_doc.get(\"Network_Role\", \"\")\n    if role != \"Master\":\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"ConnectedSlave entity may only be created on a Master node \"\n            f\"(Network_Role='Master'). Got: '{role}'\"\n        )\n    return mfdb_core_create_entity_file(\n        manifest_path=manifest_path,\n        entity_name=\"ConnectedSlave\",\n        fields=CONNECTED_SLAVE_SCHEMA,\n        description=\"Registry of Slave nodes connected to this Master.\",\n        primary_key=\"slave_id\",\n    )\n\n\n# Federation: Master -> Slave config push. Atomically drops config_doc (a BEJSON 104a document) into the slave's drop-zone path.\ndef mfdb_federation_push_config(config_doc: dict, slave_target_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Master \u2192 Slave atomic drop-zone push.\n    Writes config_doc as a BEJSON 104a document to slave_target_path using a\n    same-directory temp file + OS rename \u2014 guards against partial reads on the\n    Slave side if it polls mid-write.\n    \"\"\"\n    dest = os.path.abspath(slave_target_path)\n    dest_dir = os.path.dirname(dest)\n    os.makedirs(dest_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    fd, temp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=dest_dir, suffix=\".tmp\")\n    os.close(fd)\n    try:\n        with open(temp_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            json.dump(config_doc, f, indent=2)\n        os.rename(temp_path, dest)\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.error(f\"[MFDB_FEDERATION] push_config failed: {e}\")\n        if os.path.exists(temp_path):\n            try:\n                os.remove(temp_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n        return False\n\n\n# Federation: polls a drop-zone directory and calls callback(path, doc) for each new document, then consumes it. Blocking loop.\ndef mfdb_federation_poll_dropzone(\n    dropzone_dir: str,\n    callback: Callable[[str, dict], None],\n    poll_interval: float = 2.0,\n    timeout: float = 60.0,\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"\n    Slave: poll a local dropzone directory for incoming BEJSON 104a config docs\n    pushed by the Master. Each .bejson file found is parsed, passed to callback,\n    then removed. Runs until timeout seconds elapse.\n    Returns the count of configs processed.\n    \"\"\"\n    dropzone_p = Path(dropzone_dir)\n    dropzone_p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    processed = 0\n    deadline  = time.time() + timeout\n\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        for fpath in sorted(dropzone_p.glob(\"*.bejson\")):\n            try:\n                doc = json.loads(fpath.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n                callback(str(fpath), doc)\n                fpath.unlink()\n                processed += 1\n            except Exception as e:\n                logging.warning(f\"[MFDB_FEDERATION] poll_dropzone skipped {fpath}: {e}\")\n        time.sleep(poll_interval)\n\n    return processed\n\n\n# Federation: compacts a slave entity's log rows and delivers them to the master's poll directory.\ndef mfdb_federation_distill_logs(\n    slave_manifest_path: str,\n    entity_name: str,\n    master_poll_dir: str,\n    max_rows: int = 100,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Slave \u2192 Master one-way push (log distillation).\n    Reads the entity file, extracts rows above max_rows (oldest overflow),\n    pushes a distilled summary doc to master_poll_dir via an atomic rename,\n    then truncates the local entity file back to max_rows.\n    Returns True on success, False if nothing to distill or on error.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        entity_path = _resolve_entity_path(slave_manifest_path, entity_name)\n        doc  = _load_json(entity_path)\n        rows = doc.get(\"Values\", [])\n\n        if len(rows) <= max_rows:\n            return True  # Nothing to distill\n\n        overflow = rows[:-max_rows]\n        kept     = rows[-max_rows:]\n\n        # Push distilled summary to Master's poll dir\n        master_poll_p = Path(master_poll_dir)\n        master_poll_p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        ts      = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ\")\n        dest    = str(master_poll_p / f\"distilled_{entity_name}_{ts}.bejson\")\n\n        summary_doc = {\n            \"Format\":             \"BEJSON\",\n            \"Format_Version\":     \"104a\",\n            \"Format_Creator\":     \"Elton Boehnen\",\n            \"Distill_Source\":     entity_name,\n            \"Distill_Timestamp\":  datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            \"Records_Type\":       [\"DistilledLog\"],\n            \"Fields\":             doc.get(\"Fields\", []),\n            \"Values\":             overflow,\n        }\n\n        if not mfdb_federation_push_config(summary_doc, dest):\n            return False\n\n        # Truncate local entity to max_rows\n        doc[\"Values\"] = kept\n        bejson_core_atomic_write(entity_path, doc)\n\n        # Update record count in manifest\n        manifest_doc = _load_json(slave_manifest_path)\n        fields = manifest_doc.get(\"Fields\", [])\n        fm     = {f[\"name\"]: i for i, f in enumerate(fields)}\n        for row in manifest_doc.get(\"Values\", []):\n            if row[fm.get(\"entity_name\", 0)] == entity_name and \"record_count\" in fm:\n                row[fm[\"record_count\"]] = len(kept)\n                break\n        bejson_core_atomic_write(slave_manifest_path, manifest_doc)\n\n        return True\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logging.error(f\"[MFDB_FEDERATION] distill_logs failed: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\n# \u2500\u2500 Meta-GUID Debug Entity System \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\n# Auto-created per MFDB when debug_mode=True. Add one with --doc-cat-add.\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"\\nChunks in: {doc_name}\")\n    for cat_row in cats:\n        cat_name = cat_row[_fi(\"category_name\")]\n        chunks   = _sorted_chunks_in_cat(values, cat_name)\n        print(f\"\\n  [{cat_row[_fi('category_index_pos')]}] {cat_name}  ({len(chunks)} chunk(s))\")\n        print(f\"  {'Pos':<5} {'ID':<10} {'Label'}\")\n        print(f\"  {'\u2500'*45}\")\n        for c in chunks:\n            preview = (c[_fi(\"chunk_content\")] or \"\")[:50].replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n            if len(c[_fi(\"chunk_content\")] or \"\") > 50:\n                preview += \"\u2026\"\n            print(f\"  {c[_fi('chunk_index_pos')]:<5} {c[_fi('chunk_id')]:<10} {c[_fi('chunk_label')]}\")\n            print(f\"       {preview}\")\n\ndef cmd_doc_chunk_sel(chunk_id):\n    \"\"\"Print full content of a chunk by ID.\"\"\"\n    doc_name = _require_active_doc()\n    if not doc_name:\n        return\n    doc = _load_doc_schema(doc_name)\n    if not doc:\n        return\n    chunks = _get_chunks(doc.get(\"Values\", []))\n    for c in chunks:\n        if c[_fi(\"chunk_id\")] == chunk_id:\n            print(f\"\\n\u2500\u2500 Chunk: {c[_fi('chunk_label')]} [{chunk_id}] \u2500\u2500\")\n            print(f\"   Category: {c[_fi('parent_category')]}\")\n            print(f\"   Position: {c[_fi('chunk_index_pos')]}\")\n            print(\"\u2500\" * 50)\n            print(c[_fi(\"chunk_content\")] or \"(empty)\")\n            print(\"\u2500\" * 50)\n            return\n    print(f\"Error: Chunk ID '{chunk_id}' not found.\")\n\ndef cmd_doc_chunk_add(category_name, label=None):\n    doc_name = _require_active_doc()\n    if not doc_name:\n        return\n    doc = _load_doc_schema(doc_name)\n    if not doc:\n        return\n    values = doc.get(\"Values\", [])\n\n    #
